[Gardenia Commons] (3201029)
The soft, soothing sound of the carved marble fountain that dominates Gardenia Commons breaks the silence of day.  Bright sunlight dapples the area in a warm glow and reflects off the water pouring into the fountain, causing rainbows of ethereal light to dance across the mist that clings to the cobblestone street.  A light, thin layer of dew glistens on the wrought iron benches that encircle the fountain.  You also see a sharp-beaked drake verlok'ar that is flying around, the brass Taulramil disk engraved with runes, the Doppelganger disk, a curly-tailed red sable puppy suffused with coppery undertones, a plump cream-hued puppy with tufted floppy ears, a ruby-inlaid eelskin grimoire flying around with its feathery wings, the mist-hazed Vishra disk, the Tomme disk, the burly giantman Sulliver, a rickety raffle table with some stuff on it, a mahogany message board, a wide-mouthed wicker basket, a koa rubbish bin and a chronology portal.
Also here: Squire Legionnaire Taulramil, Doppelganger, Vishra who is sitting, Tomme
Obvious paths: north, east, south, west
